   197	    // Storage
   198	    // ====================================
   199	
   200	    IRegistry public immutable REGISTRY;
   201	    address public SUPERPAYMASTER;
   202	    address public DVT_VALIDATOR;
   203	
   204	    /// @notice Validator → registered key. `isActive` doubles as registration flag.
   205	    mapping(address => BLSValidatorKey) internal _blsKeys;
   206	
   207	    /// @notice 1-indexed slot → validator address. signerMask bit `i` (0-indexed)
   208	    ///         corresponds to validator at slot `i+1`.
   209	    mapping(uint8 => address) public validatorAtSlot;
   210	
   211	    mapping(uint256 => AggregatedSignature) public aggregatedSignatures;
   212	    mapping(uint256 => bool) public executedProposals;
   213	    /// @notice Replay guard for queueSlashWithConsensus. A queue proof commits to
   214	    ///         (operator, slashLevel, epoch, chainid); once consumed it cannot be
   215	    ///         replayed — otherwise the same signed proof could re-flag an operator
   216	    ///         after the owner cancelled the slash or after it already executed
   217	    ///         (a reusable withdraw-block DoS). A fresh, legitimate re-queue simply
   218	    ///         uses a new epoch (→ new hash).
   219	    mapping(bytes32 => bool) public usedSlashQueueHashes;
   220	    mapping(uint256 => uint256) public proposalNonces;
   221	
   222	    uint256 public minThreshold = 3; // Floor for the GENERIC executeProposal path only
   223	    uint256 public defaultThreshold = 7; // Default for the reputation consensus path
   224	    uint256 public constant MAX_VALIDATORS = 64;
   225	
   226	    /// @notice Absolute signature floor enforced inside `_checkSignatures` for
   227	    ///         EVERY verification path (a hard safety net; no path may verify
   228	    ///         below this). The generic executeProposal path layers the stricter
   229	    ///         `minThreshold` on top, so lowering the slash floor to 2 (for a
   230	    ///         2-of-3 WARNING) does NOT widen the generic path — that stays at
   231	    ///         minThreshold. Also the min a slash-table entry may be set to.
   232	    uint8 public constant SLASH_THRESHOLD_FLOOR = 2;
   233	
   234	    // ====================================
   235	    // CC-48 round-2: versioned domain separation
   236	    // ====================================
   237	    //
   238	    // Every signed pre-image on every path is
   239	    //     keccak256(abi.encode(domainSeparator(), <PATH_TAG>, ...fields))
   240	    // where
   241	    //     domainSeparator() = keccak256(abi.encode(DOMAIN_NAME, block.chainid,
   242	    //                                              address(this), address(REGISTRY)))
   243	    //
   244	    // Before this change a pre-image committed to chainid only. Two aggregators
   245	    // deployed on the SAME chain over the SAME validator keys/slots therefore
   246	    // accepted byte-identical proofs — an experiment-only deployment's signatures
   247	    // replayed verbatim against a production one. Refusing to *configure* the
   248	    // experimental contract is a policy control, not a cryptographic one; the
   249	    // domain now carries `address(this)` and the bound Registry, so a proof simply
   250	    // does not verify anywhere else. Registry re-derives the identical separator
   251	    // locally (see Registry.blsDomainSeparator) — it never asks the aggregator for
   252	    // it, keeping the second verification independent.
   253	    //
   254	    // Bump the *_v1 suffixes ONLY on a breaking encoding change; DVT/SDK pin them.
   255	
   256	    /// @notice Versioned domain name shared by every BLS pre-image in this system.
   257	    bytes32 public constant DOMAIN_NAME = keccak256("SuperPaymaster.BLSConsensus.v1");
   258	
   259	    /// @notice Path tags. Distinct per path so a proof for one path is never a
   260	    ///         valid proof for another, even with otherwise-identical fields.
   261	    bytes32 public constant TAG_QUEUE_SLASH = keccak256("SuperPaymaster.BLS.QueueSlash.v1");
   262	    bytes32 public constant TAG_EXECUTE_SLASH = keccak256("SuperPaymaster.BLS.ExecuteSlash.v1");
   263	    bytes32 public constant TAG_REPUTATION = keccak256("SuperPaymaster.BLS.Reputation.v1");
   264	    bytes32 public constant TAG_PROPOSAL = keccak256("SuperPaymaster.BLS.Proposal.v1");
   265	    bytes32 public constant TAG_POP = keccak256("SuperPaymaster.BLS.PoP.v1");
   266	    bytes32 public constant TAG_SIGNERS_COMMITMENT = keccak256("SuperPaymaster.BLS.SignersCommitment.v1");
   267	    bytes32 public constant TAG_FRAUD_PROOF = keccak256("SuperPaymaster.BLS.FraudProof.v1");
   268	
   269	    /// @notice The domain separator every pre-image on this contract commits to.
   270	    /// @dev    Not cached in storage: `block.chainid` must stay live so a chain
   271	    ///         fork cannot inherit the pre-fork domain, and both addresses are
   272	    ///         immutable anyway, so there is nothing to cache.
   273	    function domainSeparator() public view returns (bytes32) {
   274	        return keccak256(abi.encode(DOMAIN_NAME, block.chainid, address(this), address(REGISTRY)));
   275	    }
   276	
   277	    /// @notice Canonical digest handed to `IFraudProofVerifier.verify`.
   278	    /// @dev    Public so DVT can reproduce it byte-for-byte off-chain.
   279	    function fraudProofDigest(uint256 fraudProofId, address[] calldata guiltyGuardians) public view returns (bytes32) {
   280	        return keccak256(abi.encode(domainSeparator(), TAG_FRAUD_PROOF, fraudProofId, guiltyGuardians));
   281	    }
   282	
   283	    /// @notice Canonical proof-of-possession digest for (validator, publicKey).
   284	    /// @dev    CC-48 round-2 HIGH: the pre-image now binds the VALIDATOR ADDRESS
   285	    ///         (and the domain). Previously it covered only the public key, so the
   286	    ///         PoP sitting in one registrant's public calldata could be lifted by
   287	    ///         any other ROLE_DVT address to register the same key at a second slot.
   288	    ///         With the same key in N slots the reconstructed pkAgg is N*pk, and the
   289	    ///         single key holder can produce N*sk*H(m) — one signer masquerading as
   290	    ///         a quorum. Address binding kills the lift; `blsKeyOwner` (below) kills
   291	    ///         the duplicate registration even when the key holder tries it himself.
   292	    function popDigest(address validator, BLS.G1Point calldata publicKey) public view returns (bytes32) {
   293	        return keccak256(
   294	            abi.encode(
   295	                domainSeparator(), TAG_POP, validator, publicKey.x_a, publicKey.x_b, publicKey.y_a, publicKey.y_b
   296	            )
   297	        );
   298	    }
   299	
   300	    /// @notice H-1: privileged selectors the GENERIC executeProposal path may never invoke
   301	    ///         on any target (caller-chosen threshold >= minThreshold, no proposalFloor):
   302	    ///         - SP queueSlash / executeSlashWithBLS and Registry/DVT markProposalExecuted:
   303	    ///           slash and id-marking go through verifyAndExecute / queueSlashWithConsensus.
   304	    ///         - Registry.batchUpdateGlobalReputation: Registry re-verifies the proof but its
   305	    ///           domain does not change on DVT rotation and it has no floor, so a generic call
   306	    ///           could execute a pre-rotation reputation proof. Reputation goes through the
   307	    ///           verifyAndExecute reputation branch, which enforces proposalFloor.
   308	    ///         - GTokenStaking slash / slashByDVT: an authorized slasher could otherwise burn
   309	    ///           any stake by any amount, bypassing severity bps, cooldown and floor.
   310	    ///         Whole targets are NOT blocked: Registry.updateOperatorBlacklist (own proof,
   311	    ///         defaultThreshold, blacklistNonce) and SP.updatePriceDVT (freshness, ±20% bands)
   312	    ///         are legitimate generic actions. scripts/check_generic_selector_coverage.py (CI)
   313	    ///         classifies every privileged selector reachable from this path.
   314	    bytes4 private constant SEL_QUEUE_SLASH = bytes4(keccak256("queueSlash(address)"));
   315	    bytes4 private constant SEL_EXECUTE_SLASH_BLS = bytes4(keccak256("executeSlashWithBLS(address,uint8,bytes)"));
   316	    bytes4 private constant SEL_MARK_PROPOSAL_EXECUTED = bytes4(keccak256("markProposalExecuted(uint256)"));
   317	    bytes4 private constant SEL_BATCH_REPUTATION =
   318	        bytes4(keccak256("batchUpdateGlobalReputation(uint256,address[],uint256[],uint256,bytes)"));
   319	    bytes4 private constant SEL_STAKING_SLASH = bytes4(keccak256("slash(address,uint256,string)"));
   320	    bytes4 private constant SEL_STAKING_SLASH_BY_DVT = bytes4(keccak256("slashByDVT(address,bytes32,uint256,string)"));
   321	
   322	    /// @notice Per-severity slash consensus threshold, keyed by SlashLevel
   323	    ///         (0=WARNING, 1=MINOR, 2=MAJOR). Bootstrap (N=3): 2/3/3. This
   324	    ///         replaces the flat defaultThreshold for the slash-only path so the
   325	    ///         bar scales with severity and with the validator set over time,
   326	    ///         by governance table update rather than a code change.
   327	    mapping(uint8 => uint8) public slashThresholds;
   328	
   329	    /// @notice Address permitted to update `slashThresholds`. Set this to a
   330	    ///         multisig for plain governance, or to a TimelockController(multisig)
   331	    ///         to get timelocked policy changes WITHOUT building timelock logic
   332	    ///         (and its bytecode) into this contract. Owner rotates it.
   333	    address public slashPolicyAdmin;
   334	
   335	    /// @notice Fraction of a colluding guardian's remaining ROLE_DVT lock burned per
   336	    ///         `executeGuardianSlash`, in basis points.
   337	    /// @dev    WHY THIS EXISTS. Before it, this path passed the guardian's ENTIRE remaining
   338	    ///         lock to `slashByDVT`, so `lock.amount == 0` was hit on every successful
   339	    ///         guardian slash and `_removeUserRole` fired every time: the contract could not
   340	    ///         express a partial slash at all. With registrations sitting exactly at
   341	    ///         `minStake` and the MINOR threshold equal to the registered count, one slash
   342	    ///         therefore dropped the slash quorum below its own threshold — the punishment
   343	    ///         mechanism disabling the mechanism. Over-staking could not fix that while the
   344	    ///         penalty was defined as "everything left".
   345	    ///
   346	    ///         WHAT THIS DOES AND DOES NOT FIX. A fraction makes over-staking meaningful,
   347	    ///         and that is all it does. It does NOT on its own stop the quorum from
   348	    ///         disabling itself, because the eligibility gate is an absolute comparison
   349	    ///         against `minStake`, not a relative one:
   350	    ///
   351	    ///           stake == minStake  (the CURRENT deployment: every ROLE_DVT lock is
   352	    ///                               exactly 30e18)      -> 30% slash leaves 0.7x minStake
   353	    ///                                                      -> still below the gate
   354	    ///                                                      -> still ejected
   355	    ///                                                      -> quorum still collapses
   356	    ///           stake >= minStake / (1 - bps)  (>= ~1.43x, i.e. 43e18 for a 30e18 gate)
   357	    ///                                              -> survives one slash, stays in quorum
   358	    ///
   359	    ///         So the cap is NECESSARY and NOT SUFFICIENT. The other half is an operational
   360	    ///         rule — stake above `minStake` — which nothing in this contract enforces.
   361	    ///         `GuardianSlashE2E` pins both halves: the margin-0 case still ejects, and the
   362	    ///         over-staked case does not. Do not read this parameter as having solved it.
   363	    ///
   364	    ///         It also does NOT make a guardian un-slashable: repeated collusion walks the
   365	    ///         balance down, and each case is a separate `fraudProofId`.
   366	    uint16 public guardianSlashBps;
   367	
   368	    /// @dev Fat-finger bounds, not policy. 0 would make the path a no-op that still marks
   369	    ///      the case resolved; above 100% is meaningless. Ops pick the real value.
   370	    uint16 public constant GUARDIAN_SLASH_BPS_MIN = 100; // 1%
   371	    uint16 public constant GUARDIAN_SLASH_BPS_MAX = 10000; // 100%
   372	    uint16 internal constant _BPS_DENOMINATOR = 10000;
   373	
   374	    /// @notice H-02: when true, a staked ROLE_DVT validator may self-register their OWN
   375	    ///         BLS key (with proof-of-possession) instead of requiring an owner call.
   376	    ///         Default false — onboarding stays owner-gated (off-chain trust established
   377	    ///         first) until governance flips it on. Closes the otherwise-inconsistent
   378	    ///         path where Registry ROLE_DVT is permissionless (self-service stake) but
   379	    ///         BLS-key registration here was owner-only.
   380	    bool public permissionlessBLSRegistration;
   381	
   382	    /// @notice DVT-supplied fraud-proof verifier for guardian-collusion slashing
   383	    ///         (Protocol B stage 2). address(0) = feature dormant: NO NEW CASE can be
   384	    ///         opened, because `queueGuardianSlash` — the only way to open one — reverts
   385	    ///         with `FraudProofVerifierNotSet`. It does NOT stop a case that is already
   386	    ///         queued: since round-4 the verdict is frozen at queue time, so
   387	    ///         `executeGuardianSlash` never reads this slot at all (CC-48 round-5
   388	    ///         MEDIUM-2: the pre-round-5 NatSpec claimed the opposite and would have led
   389	    ///         an operator to believe zeroing this halts an in-flight slash).
   390	    ///         This is the ONE seam the detection layer plugs into; the aggregator never
   391	    ///         judges fraud itself.
   392	    address public fraudProofVerifier;
   393	
   394	    /// @notice Per-(fraudProofId, guardian) slash record for executeGuardianSlash.
   395	    ///         Consumption is tracked PER GUARDIAN — and only for guardians actually
   396	    ///         slashed — so submitting an already-exited co-signer can never burn the
   397	    ///         proof for the still-staked colluders (the global-id-consumption flaw this
   398	    ///         replaces). Own id-space; never collides with slash/reputation proposalIds.
   399	    mapping(uint256 => mapping(address => bool)) public guardianSlashed;
   400	
   401	    /// @notice A' attribution (CC-89 stage-2): commitment to the exact signer
   402	    ///         ADDRESS set of each executed proposal, snapshotted at execution time
   403	    ///         before any revokeBLSPublicKey can reassign a slot. A fraud-proof
   404	    ///         verifier matches proof-supplied claimedSigners against this to
   405	    ///         attribute a fraudulent slash to real addresses. It is a 1-slot
   406	    ///         fingerprint and is NOT reversible — the address list's data
   407	    ///         availability is the DVT detection layer's (redundant watchers) job.
   408	    mapping(uint256 => bytes32) public proposalSignersCommitment;
   409	
   410	    /// @notice Two-step guardian-slash lifecycle. Queueing a verifier-approved
   411	    ///         case freezes every accused guardian's ROLE_DVT exit in Registry;
   412	    ///         execution or permissionless expiry releases exactly one count.
   413	    mapping(uint256 => GuardianSlashCase) public guardianSlashCases;
   414	    mapping(address => GuardianExitRequest) public guardianExitRequests;
   415	    mapping(address => uint256) public pendingGuardianSlashCount;
   416	
   417	    /// @notice CC-48 HIGH-2: per-(case, guardian) release marker. Set exactly once,
   418	    ///         when that guardian's `pendingGuardianSlashCount` contribution for the
   419	    ///         case is given back — either because the slash succeeded, because the
   420	    ///         guardian had nothing left to slash, or because the case expired. A
   421	    ///         guardian whose `slashByDVT` reverted stays UNresolved and frozen, so a
   422	    ///         single staking-side failure can no longer release the whole set.
   423	    mapping(uint256 => mapping(address => bool)) public guardianCaseResolved;
   424	
   425	    /// @notice CC-48 round-2: persistent binding of a G1 public key to the FIRST
   426	    ///         validator address it was registered under.
   427	    /// @dev    Retained on revocation and exit; owner-only releaseKeyBinding may clear
   428	    ///         it only when no active registration uses that key.
   429	    ///         Clearing it would let a revoked key be re-claimed by a different
   430	    ///         address, which is exactly the duplicate-key condition this prevents.
   431	    ///         A validator re-registering its OWN key (rotation back, slot reuse)
   432	    ///         still passes because the binding matches.
   433	    ///         Key: keccak256(abi.encode(pk.x_a, pk.x_b, pk.y_a, pk.y_b)).
   434	    mapping(bytes32 => address) public blsKeyOwner;
   435	
   436	    /// @notice Monotonic lower bound on allocation and execution through the current
   437	    ///         DVT (slash and aggregator-routed reputation), retained across rotations.
   438	    /// @dev Owner-direct verifyAndExecute with caller-chosen ids, generic proposals,
   439	    ///      authorized direct Registry reputation proofs and unused consensus queue
   440	    ///      pre-flags are outside this execution floor.
   441	    uint256 public proposalFloor;
   442	    /// @notice Authoritative suspension, independent of key activity or DVT admission.
   443	    mapping(address => bool) public suspended;
   444	    event SuspensionUpdated(address indexed validator, bool suspended);
   445	    error ValidatorSuspended(address validator);
   446	
   447	    /// @notice CC-48 BLOCKER-1: earliest timestamp at which a guardian may open a new
   448	    ///         exit notice after cancelling one. Kills request/cancel flip-flopping as
   449	    ///         a cheap, repeatable lever on the signer set.
   450	    mapping(address => uint64) public guardianExitCooldownUntil;
   451	
   452	    /// @notice CC-48 MEDIUM-1: two-step, delay-guarded fraud-proof verifier rotation.
   453	    ///         `pendingFraudProofVerifierReadyAt != 0` means a rotation is in flight.
   454	    address public pendingFraudProofVerifier;
   455	    uint64 public pendingFraudProofVerifierReadyAt;
   456	
   457	    /// @dev CC-48 HIGH-2: the case window MUST strictly dominate a full exit notice
   458	    ///      (delay + challenge + consumption window). 5 > 2 + 1 + 1 ensures a
   459	    ///      notice open when a case is queued expires before that case does.
   460	    ///      Pending cases block new notices and cancellation.
   461	    uint256 public constant GUARDIAN_SLASH_CASE_WINDOW = 5 days;
   462	    uint256 public constant GUARDIAN_EXIT_DELAY = 2 days;
   463	    uint256 public constant GUARDIAN_FRAUD_CHALLENGE = 1 days;
   464	    uint256 public constant GUARDIAN_EXIT_WINDOW = 1 days;
   465	    /// @notice Quiet period imposed after cancelling an exit notice (BLOCKER-1).
   466	    uint256 public constant GUARDIAN_EXIT_COOLDOWN = 1 days;
   467	    /// @notice Verifier rotations mature no faster than a full case window, so
   468	    ///         governance cannot retroactively kill a queued case by swapping in a
   469	    ///         verifier that returns false (CC-48 MEDIUM-1).
   470	    uint256 public constant VERIFIER_ROTATION_DELAY = GUARDIAN_SLASH_CASE_WINDOW;
   680	    error DuplicatePublicKey(bytes32 keyHash, address boundTo);
   681	    /// @dev CC-48 round-2: the combined (reputation + slash) proposal shape is
   682	    ///      rejected outright. Its aggregator pre-image committed to the real
   683	    ///      operator/slashLevel while Registry re-derived the reputation pre-image
   684	    ///      with address(0)/0, so the two verifications could never agree on the same
   685	    ///      signature. Rather than invent a second reputation schema, the shape is
   686	    ///      forbidden: submit the slash and the reputation update as separate proposals.
   687	    error CombinedProposalNotSupported();
   688	
   689	    // ====================================
   690	    // Constructor
   691	    // ====================================
   692	
   693	    constructor(address _registry, address _superPaymaster, address _dvtValidator) Ownable(msg.sender) {
   694	        if (_registry == address(0)) revert InvalidAddress(_registry);
   695	        if (_superPaymaster == address(0)) revert InvalidAddress(_superPaymaster);
   696	        if (_dvtValidator == address(0)) revert InvalidAddress(_dvtValidator);
   697	        REGISTRY = IRegistry(_registry);
   698	        SUPERPAYMASTER = _superPaymaster;
   699	        DVT_VALIDATOR = _dvtValidator;
   700	
   701	        // Bootstrap slash thresholds for N=3: WARNING 2-of-3 (no funds moved,
   702	        // reputation ding only), MINOR/MAJOR 3-of-3 (real aPNTs at stake).
   703	        // Governance raises these via setSlashThreshold as the validator set grows.
   704	        slashThresholds[uint8(ISuperPaymasterSlash.SlashLevel.WARNING)] = 2;
   705	        slashThresholds[uint8(ISuperPaymasterSlash.SlashLevel.MINOR)] = 3;
   706	        slashThresholds[uint8(ISuperPaymasterSlash.SlashLevel.MAJOR)] = 3;
   707	
   708	        // Owner is the initial policy admin; rotate to a multisig / timelock post-deploy.
   709	        slashPolicyAdmin = msg.sender;
   710	        // 30%, mirroring SuperPaymaster's automated-slash hardcap. That cap protects the
   711	        // operator's aPNTs balance; this one protects the guardian's GToken lock, which is
   712	        // the asset the committee-eligibility gate actually reads.
   713	        guardianSlashBps = 3000;
   714	    }
   715	
   716	    /// @notice Update the per-severity slash consensus threshold.
   717	    /// @dev    Gated to `slashPolicyAdmin` (a multisig, or a TimelockController for
   718	    ///         timelocked changes). Floored at SLASH_THRESHOLD_FLOOR and capped at
   719	    ///         MAX_VALIDATORS. Operationally the value should stay <= the active
   720	    ///         validator count, otherwise that severity becomes unslashable.
   721	    function setSlashThreshold(uint8 slashLevel, uint8 threshold) external {
   722	        if (msg.sender != slashPolicyAdmin) revert NotSlashPolicyAdmin(msg.sender);
   723	        if (slashLevel > uint8(ISuperPaymasterSlash.SlashLevel.MAJOR)) revert InvalidParameter("slashLevel");
   724	        if (threshold < SLASH_THRESHOLD_FLOOR || threshold > MAX_VALIDATORS) {
   725	            revert SlashThresholdOutOfRange(threshold);
   726	        }
   727	        emit SlashThresholdUpdated(slashLevel, slashThresholds[slashLevel], threshold);
   728	        slashThresholds[slashLevel] = threshold;
   729	    }
   730	
   731	    /// @notice Set the fraction of a colluding guardian's lock burned per slash.
   732	    /// @dev    Gated to `slashPolicyAdmin` exactly like `setSlashThreshold`: both decide how
   733	    ///         hard an automated path can hit someone's stake, so they share one admin.
   734	    function setGuardianSlashBps(uint16 newBps) external {
   735	        if (msg.sender != slashPolicyAdmin) revert NotSlashPolicyAdmin(msg.sender);
   736	        if (newBps < GUARDIAN_SLASH_BPS_MIN || newBps > GUARDIAN_SLASH_BPS_MAX) {
   737	            revert InvalidParameter("guardianSlashBps");
   738	        }
   739	        emit GuardianSlashBpsUpdated(guardianSlashBps, newBps);
   740	        guardianSlashBps = newBps;
   741	    }
   742	
   743	    /// @notice Rotate the slash-policy admin (owner only).
   744	    function setSlashPolicyAdmin(address newAdmin) external onlyOwner {
   745	        if (newAdmin == address(0)) revert InvalidAddress(newAdmin);
   746	        emit SlashPolicyAdminUpdated(slashPolicyAdmin, newAdmin);
   747	        slashPolicyAdmin = newAdmin;
   748	    }
   749	
   750	    // ====================================
   751	    // Core Functions
   752	    // ====================================
   753	
   754	    /// @notice Register a BLS validator's public key into a deterministic slot.
   755	    /// @dev    P0-1: keys are stored uncompressed so `_reconstructPkAgg` can
   756	    ///         feed them straight into the G1ADD precompile. The slot encodes
   757	    ///         the validator's bit position in `signerMask` and is fixed at
   758	    ///         registration to make the bitmap → key mapping unambiguous.
   759	    ///
   760	    ///         P0-1 sub-fix (on-curve + subgroup check): `_validateG1Point` is
   761	    ///         called before storing to guarantee (a) the point is on the
   762	    ///         BLS12-381 G1 curve and (b) it is in the prime-order subgroup r.
   763	    ///         Without (b) an attacker can register a small-subgroup point that
   764	    ///         contaminates the reconstructed pkAgg used in later pairing checks.
   765	    ///         The identity point (point at infinity) is also rejected to prevent
   766	    ///         key-cancellation attacks during aggregation.
   767	    /// @param  validator  validator address (used for events / dedup).
   768	    /// @param  publicKey  uncompressed EIP-2537 G1 point (4×32 bytes).
   769	    /// @param  slot       1-indexed slot in [1..MAX_VALIDATORS]. Must not collide
   770	    ///                    with another validator's already-bound slot.
   771	    /// @param  popSignature proof-of-possession (G2): the validator's BLS signature over
   772	    ///                    `popDigest(validator, publicKey)`. REQUIRED on BOTH paths
   773	    ///                    (CC-48 round-3); a registration without a valid PoP reverts.
   774	    ///
   775	    /// @dev SECURITY: Only the current DVT may forward registration. Both owner
   776	    ///      onboarding and permissionless onboarding require PoP on every call,
   777	    ///      bound to this aggregator, chain, validator address and public key.
   778	    ///      Suspension, key validity, uniqueness and slot consistency are enforced
   779	    ///      here. Key bindings survive revoke/exit unless the owner explicitly
   780	    ///      releases an unused binding (see releaseKeyBinding recovery runbook).
   781	    ///      Aggregation re-checks live role, stake, suspension and matured exits;
   782	    ///      a stored key alone never establishes current voting eligibility.
   783	    function registerBLSPublicKey(
   784	        address validator,
   785	        BLS.G1Point calldata publicKey,
   786	        uint8 slot,
   787	        BLS.G2Point calldata popSignature
   788	    ) external {
   789	        if (msg.sender != DVT_VALIDATOR) revert UnauthorizedCaller(msg.sender);
   790	        if (suspended[validator]) revert ValidatorSuspended(validator);
   791	        if (validator == address(0)) revert InvalidAddress(address(0));
   792	        if (slot == 0 || slot > MAX_VALIDATORS) revert SlotOutOfRange(slot);
   793	
   794	        // CC-48 round-3: proof-of-possession is MANDATORY on BOTH paths. On-curve +
   795	        // prime-order subgroup membership is validated first, so `_verifyPoP` never
   796	        // pairs against a small-subgroup point.
   797	        _validateG1Point(publicKey);
   798	        if (!_verifyPoP(validator, publicKey, popSignature)) revert InvalidPoP();
   799	
   800	        // CC-48 round-2: one public key, one validator address until owner-only release. Enforced on
   801	        // the OWNER path too: the owner is trusted to curate keys, not to be immune to
   802	        // a copy-paste, and a duplicated key silently multiplies one signer's weight.
   803	        bytes32 keyHash = keccak256(abi.encode(publicKey.x_a, publicKey.x_b, publicKey.y_a, publicKey.y_b));
   804	        address keyOwner = blsKeyOwner[keyHash];
   805	        if (keyOwner == address(0)) {
   806	            blsKeyOwner[keyHash] = validator;
   807	        } else if (keyOwner != validator) {
   808	            revert DuplicatePublicKey(keyHash, keyOwner);
   809	        }
   810	
   811	        BLSValidatorKey storage existing = _blsKeys[validator];
   812	        // Re-registration of the SAME validator must reuse the prior slot to
   813	        // avoid leaving a dangling slot pointer for an already-active mask bit.
   814	        if (existing.isActive && existing.index != slot) revert SlotAlreadyTaken(slot);
   815	
   816	        // The slot must either be free, or currently occupied by this same validator.
   817	        address current = validatorAtSlot[slot];
   818	        if (current != address(0) && current != validator) revert SlotAlreadyTaken(slot);
   819	
   820	        _blsKeys[validator] = BLSValidatorKey({publicKey: publicKey, index: slot, isActive: true});
   821	        validatorAtSlot[slot] = validator;
   822	        emit BLSPublicKeyRegistered(validator, slot);
   823	    }
   824	
   825	    /// @notice Revoke a registered key. Direct owner revocation rejects inactive keys.
   826	    /// @dev Current DVT removal is an owner-forwarded suspension plus revocation,
   827	    ///      preserves each reachable configured quorum, and is idempotent for an
   828	    ///      inactive key. The DVT checks that its caller is also this contract's owner.
   829	    ///      Direct owner calls are immediate emergency overrides; no timelock is
   830	    ///      enforced here. Use revokeAndSuspend to prevent permissionless restoration.
   831	    ///      Old key bytes remain inspectable only until a subsequent registration
   832	    ///      overwrites them; getBLSPublicKey is not a historical key archive.
   833	    function revokeBLSPublicKey(address validator) external {
   834	        if (msg.sender != owner() && msg.sender != DVT_VALIDATOR) revert UnauthorizedCaller(msg.sender);
   835	        if (msg.sender == DVT_VALIDATOR) {
   836	            _requireCommitteeSurvivesExit(validator);
   837	            _setSuspended(validator, true);
   838	        } else if (!_blsKeys[validator].isActive) {
   839	            revert KeyNotActive(validator);
   840	        }
   841	        _revokeBLSPublicKey(validator);
   842	    }
   843	
   844	    /// @notice Set or clear suspension regardless of key or admission state.
   845	    function setSuspended(address validator, bool value) external onlyOwner {
   846	        _setSuspended(validator, value);
   847	    }
   848	
   849	    /// @notice Atomic emergency eviction; bypasses quorum survival and works without a key.
   850	    function revokeAndSuspend(address validator) external onlyOwner {
   851	        _setSuspended(validator, true);
   852	        _revokeBLSPublicKey(validator);
   853	    }
   854	
   855	    function _setSuspended(address validator, bool value) internal {
   856	        suspended[validator] = value;
   857	        emit SuspensionUpdated(validator, value);
   858	    }
   859	
   860	    function _revokeBLSPublicKey(address validator) internal {
   861	        BLSValidatorKey storage existing = _blsKeys[validator];
   862	        if (!existing.isActive) return;
   863	        uint8 slot = existing.index;
   864	        existing.isActive = false;
   865	        validatorAtSlot[slot] = address(0);
   866	        emit BLSPublicKeyRevoked(validator, slot);
   867	    }
   868	
   869	    /// @notice CC-48 round-3 recovery: release a `blsKeyOwner` binding that no ACTIVE
   870	    ///         slot is using.
   871	    /// @dev    The binding is intentionally permanent on the normal paths — clearing it
   872	    ///         on `revokeBLSPublicKey` would let a revoked key be re-claimed by a
   873	    ///         different address, which is exactly the duplicate-key condition the
   874	    ///         binding exists to prevent. This function is the ONE escape hatch for a
   875	    ///         binding created in error, and it is deliberately narrow:
   876	    ///           - owner-only (governance action, not a validator-facing one), and
   877	    ///           - refuses while ANY active slot holds this key, so a live signer's
   878	    ///             binding can never be released out from under it. Combined with the
   879	    ///             now-mandatory PoP on both registration paths, the only way to reach
   880	    ///             this function's precondition is a key that is registered nowhere.
   881	    ///         Runbook: revoke the slot first (`revokeBLSPublicKey`), confirm
   882	    ///         `getBLSPublicKey(...).isActive == false` for every holder, then release.
   883	    ///         After release the key may be re-registered by whoever can produce a PoP
   884	    ///         for it — i.e. its actual holder.
   885	    /// @param  keyHash keccak256(abi.encode(pk.x_a, pk.x_b, pk.y_a, pk.y_b)).
   886	    function releaseKeyBinding(bytes32 keyHash) external onlyOwner {
   887	        address boundTo = blsKeyOwner[keyHash];
   888	        if (boundTo == address(0)) revert InvalidParameter("keyHash");
   889	        // Registration admits this key only for boundTo. Until this binding is
   890	        // released, no other address can hold it, even after revocation or rotation.
   891	        BLSValidatorKey storage k = _blsKeys[boundTo];
   892	        if (k.isActive && keccak256(abi.encode(k.publicKey)) == keyHash) {
   893	            revert KeyBindingStillActive(keyHash, boundTo);
   894	        }
   895	        delete blsKeyOwner[keyHash];
   896	        emit BLSKeyBindingReleased(keyHash, boundTo);
   897	    }
   898	
   899	    /// @notice View accessor returning the stored G1 public key + slot for a validator.
   900	    function getBLSPublicKey(address validator)
   901	        external
   902	        view
   903	        returns (BLS.G1Point memory publicKey, uint8 slot, bool isActive)
   904	    {
   905	        BLSValidatorKey memory k = _blsKeys[validator];
   906	        return (k.publicKey, k.index, k.isActive);
   907	    }
   908	
   909	    /// @notice External BLS pairing verification used by Registry / ReputationSystem.
   910	    /// @dev    P0-1: callers cannot supply pkAgg or msgG2 anymore. Both are
   911	    ///         derived deterministically — pkAgg from `signerMask` against the
   912	    ///         on-chain validator set, msgG2 from `expectedMessageHash`. Returns
   913	    ///         true iff the pairing equation holds and at least
   914	    ///         `requiredThreshold` distinct on-chain validators are selected.
   915	    /// @param  expectedMessageHash The exact hash the signers committed to.
   916	    /// @param  signerMask Bitmask of signing validator slots (bit i = slot i+1).
   917	    /// @param  requiredThreshold Caller's minimum signer count requirement.
   918	    /// @param  sigBytes abi.encode(BLS.G2Point) of the aggregated G2 signature.
   919	    function verify(bytes32 expectedMessageHash, uint256 signerMask, uint256 requiredThreshold, bytes calldata sigBytes)
   920	        external
   921	        view
   922	        returns (bool)
   923	    {
   924	        if (signerMask == 0) revert EmptySignerMask();
   925	        if (requiredThreshold < minThreshold) {
   926	            revert InvalidThreshold();
   927	        }
   928	        if (requiredThreshold > MAX_VALIDATORS) {
   929	            revert InvalidThreshold();
   930	        }
   931	
   932	        (BLS.G1Point memory pkAgg, uint256 signerCount) = _reconstructPkAgg(signerMask);
   933	        if (signerCount < requiredThreshold) {
   934	            revert InvalidSignatureCount(signerCount, requiredThreshold);
   935	        }
   936	
   937	        BLS.G2Point memory sig = abi.decode(sigBytes, (BLS.G2Point));
   938	        BLS.G2Point memory msgG2 = BLS.hashToG2(abi.encodePacked(expectedMessageHash));
   939	
   940	        BLS.G1Point[] memory g1s = new BLS.G1Point[](2);
   941	        BLS.G2Point[] memory g2s = new BLS.G2Point[](2);
   942	        g1s[0] = _getG1Generator();
   943	        g2s[0] = sig;
   944	        g1s[1] = _negateG1Point(pkAgg);
   945	        g2s[1] = msgG2;
   946	
   947	        return BLS.pairing(g1s, g2s);
   948	    }
   949	
   950	    /// @notice Step 1 of the two-step slash: a DVT quorum pre-flags `operator` for
   951	    ///         slashing (SP.queueSlash), blocking their withdraw until the slash is
   952	    ///         executed (verifyAndExecute) or the owner cancels it. Kept SEPARATE
   953	    ///         from execution so the flag lands in an earlier tx — closing the
   954	    ///         front-run window an atomic queue+execute would reopen.
   955	    /// @dev    Dedicated (not the generic executeProposal) so it (a) is gated at the
   956	    ///         per-severity slash threshold, (b) does NOT consume a proposalId /
   957	    ///         executedProposals slot, leaving the execute-step proposalId intact (a
   958	    ///         BLS re-queue is NOT a no-op on SP: it refreshes slashQueues provenance and
   959	    ///         timestamp, emits again, and may revert during the BLS cooldown), and (c) requires a
   960	    ///         real quorum, so no single validator can DoS an operator's withdraw.
   961	    ///         The queue message is domain-separated ("QUEUE_SLASH") so a queue proof
   962	    ///         can never be replayed as an execute proof.
   963	    ///         Accepted residual: unused pre-rotation queue proofs remain valid. The
   964	    ///         pre-flag moves no funds and is always owner-cancellable via
   965	    ///         SuperPaymasterAdmin.cancelSlash. A BLS-queued freeze has no time expiry
   966	    ///         in SuperPaymasterStorage._isSlashPending; GUARDIAN_SLASH_FREEZE expires
   967	    ///         only guardian queues. usedSlashQueueHashes makes each (operator,
   968	    ///         level, epoch) one-shot. BLS fund movement requires a separate slash
   969	    ///         proof; execution through the current DVT requires an id at or above
   970	    ///         proposalFloor. Owner-direct execution with caller-chosen ids is exempt.
   971	    function queueSlashWithConsensus(address operator, uint8 slashLevel, uint256 epoch, bytes calldata proof)
   972	        external
   973	        nonReentrant
   974	    {
   975	        if (msg.sender != DVT_VALIDATOR && msg.sender != owner()) {
   976	            revert UnauthorizedCaller(msg.sender);
   977	        }
   978	        if (operator == address(0)) revert InvalidTarget(operator);
   979	        if (slashLevel > uint8(ISuperPaymasterSlash.SlashLevel.MAJOR)) revert InvalidParameter("slashLevel");
   980	        uint256 requiredThreshold = slashThresholds[slashLevel];
   981	        bytes32 expectedMessageHash =
   982	            keccak256(abi.encode(domainSeparator(), TAG_QUEUE_SLASH, operator, slashLevel, epoch));
   983	        // Replay guard: a consumed queue proof cannot re-flag the operator after a
   984	        // cancel/execute cleared the flag. A legitimate re-queue uses a new epoch.
   985	        if (usedSlashQueueHashes[expectedMessageHash]) revert SlashQueueProofAlreadyUsed(expectedMessageHash);
   986	        _checkSignatures(proof, expectedMessageHash, requiredThreshold);
   987	        uint256 signerMask = abi.decode(proof[:32], (uint256));
   988	        _commitSigners(uint256(expectedMessageHash), signerMask, expectedMessageHash);
   989	        usedSlashQueueHashes[expectedMessageHash] = true;
   990	        ISuperPaymasterSlash(SUPERPAYMASTER).queueSlash(operator);
   991	        emit SlashPreQueued(operator, slashLevel, epoch, requiredThreshold);
   992	    }
   993	
   994	    /// @notice Execute slash or reputation consensus with a floor on the current DVT path.
   995	    /// @dev Rotation invalidates earlier DVT allocations on both branches, even if
   996	    ///      their allocating DVT becomes current again. Owner-direct execution with
   997	    ///      caller-chosen ids is the documented owner-mediated residual. All callers
   998	    ///      still require nonzero ids below 2^128. Direct Registry reputation proofs
   999	    ///      retain their source gate, per-user epoch and +/-100 score-delta bounds.
  1000	    function verifyAndExecute(
  1001	        uint256 proposalId,
  1002	        address operator,
  1003	        uint8 slashLevel,
  1004	        address[] calldata repUsers,
  1005	        uint256[] calldata newScores,
  1006	        uint256 epoch,
  1007	        bytes32 evidenceHash,
  1008	        bytes calldata proof
  1009	    ) external nonReentrant {
  1010	        if (msg.sender != DVT_VALIDATOR && msg.sender != owner()) {
  1011	            revert UnauthorizedCaller(msg.sender);
  1012	        }
  1013	        // Raw ids (slash AND reputation) live below 2^128, providing 128-bit
  1014	        // cryptographic separation from generic and queue keccak keys. Only the DVT
  1015	        // path uses DVT-allocated ids; owner-direct ids are caller-chosen.
  1016	        if (proposalId == 0 || proposalId >> 128 != 0 || (msg.sender == DVT_VALIDATOR && proposalId < proposalFloor)) {
  1017	            revert InvalidProposalId();
  1018	        }
  1019	        if (executedProposals[proposalId]) {
  1020	            revert ProposalAlreadyExecuted(proposalId);
  1021	        }
  1022	        // CC-48 round-2: reject the combined shape before any signature work. See
  1023	        // CombinedProposalNotSupported — the aggregator and Registry pre-images for a
  1024	        // reputation batch can only agree when operator/slashLevel are absent, so a
  1025	        // reputation proposal carrying a slash target was never executable anyway
  1026	        // (it reverted deep inside Registry with an opaque BLSFailed). Failing here,
  1027	        // by name, turns a silent dead path into an explicit protocol rule.
  1028	        if (repUsers.length != 0 && (operator != address(0) || slashLevel != 0)) {
  1029	            revert CombinedProposalNotSupported();
  1030	        }
  1031	        // CC-48 round-3 LOW: pin the slash-only shape from the OTHER side too. The
  1032	        // slash pre-image commits to (proposalId, operator, slashLevel, epoch,
  1033	        // evidenceHash) and NOT to `newScores`, so a non-empty `newScores` here would be
  1034	        // caller-controlled input that passed a signature check without being covered by
  1035	        // any signature. It is unused on this branch today; requiring it empty means it
  1036	        // can never become used-but-unsigned by a later edit.
  1037	        if (repUsers.length == 0) {
  1038	            if (newScores.length != 0) revert CombinedProposalNotSupported();
  1039	            // A proposal with no batch, no target and no severity only burns a
  1040	            // proposalId in this contract and in Registry. It needs a real quorum, so it
  1041	            // is not an attack surface — but a no-op shape on a consensus path is a
  1042	            // place for future divergence, and CC-48 is about making the protocol rules
  1043	            // explicit rather than incidental.
  1044	            if (operator == address(0) && slashLevel == 0) revert EmptyProposalNotSupported();
  1045	        }
  1046	
  1047	        // 1. Construct expected message + resolve the required threshold.
  1048	        // The signed message MUST commit to chainid to prevent cross-chain replay.
  1049	        bytes32 expectedMessageHash;
  1050	        uint256 requiredThreshold;
  1051	        if (repUsers.length == 0) {
  1052	            // Slash-only consensus path: bar = per-severity governance table
  1053	            // (2/3/3 at bootstrap), and the signed message additionally commits to
  1054	            // `evidenceHash` so every slash is bound on-chain to the off-chain
  1055	            // evidence that justified it. Keeps the reputation branch (below)
  1056	            // byte-identical so Registry's independent re-verification still matches.
  1057	            if (slashLevel > uint8(ISuperPaymasterSlash.SlashLevel.MAJOR)) revert InvalidParameter("slashLevel");
  1058	            requiredThreshold = slashThresholds[slashLevel];
  1059	            expectedMessageHash = keccak256(
  1060	                abi.encode(domainSeparator(), TAG_EXECUTE_SLASH, proposalId, operator, slashLevel, epoch, evidenceHash)
  1061	            );
  1062	        } else {
  1063	            // Reputation-only path: six top-level fields +
  1064	            // defaultThreshold, so Registry.batchUpdateGlobalReputation's
  1065	            // re-verification reconstructs the identical hash.
  1066	            requiredThreshold = defaultThreshold;
  1067	            // Byte-identical to Registry._reputationMessageHash. operator/slashLevel
  1068	            // are NOT fields here: they are pinned to zero by the combined-shape guard
  1069	            // above, so including them could only re-introduce the divergence.
  1070	            expectedMessageHash = _reputationMessageHash(proposalId, repUsers, newScores, epoch);
  1071	        }
  1072	
  1073	        // 2. Verify BLS pairing using on-chain reconstructed pkAgg (P0-1).
  1074	        _checkSignatures(proof, expectedMessageHash, requiredThreshold);
  1075	
  1076	        // 2b. A' attribution snapshot (CC-89 stage-2): commit to the signer ADDRESS
  1077	        //     set NOW — after signatures verified, before any revoke can reassign a
  1078	        //     slot — so a later fraud proof can attribute this proposal to the real
  1079	        //     addresses that signed it. Covers BOTH branches (slash-only + rep).
  1080	        uint256 signerMask = abi.decode(proof[:32], (uint256));
  1081	        _commitSigners(proposalId, signerMask, expectedMessageHash);
  1082	
  1083	        // 3. Update Global Reputation in Registry
  1084	        if (repUsers.length > 0) {
  1085	            REGISTRY.batchUpdateGlobalReputation(proposalId, repUsers, newScores, epoch, proof);
  1086	            emit ReputationEpochTriggered(epoch, repUsers.length);
  1087	        } else {
  1088	            // Slash-only proposal: mark proposalId in Registry to prevent cross-path replay
  1089	            // (attacker holding valid proof cannot reuse proposalId via direct Registry call)
  1090	            REGISTRY.markProposalExecuted(proposalId);
  1091	            emit SlashConsensusReached(proposalId, slashLevel, requiredThreshold, evidenceHash);
  1092	        }
  1093	
  1094	        // 4. Execute Slash if operator is provided
  1095	        if (operator != address(0)) {
  1096	            _executeSlash(proposalId, operator, slashLevel, proof);
  1097	        }
  1098	
  1099	        executedProposals[proposalId] = true;
  1100	        if (DVT_VALIDATOR != address(0)) {
  1101	            IDVTValidator(DVT_VALIDATOR).markProposalExecuted(proposalId);
  1102	        }
  1103	    }
  1104	
  1105	    /**
  1106	     * @notice Execute any proposal via BLS consensus (Generic DVT)
  1107	     * @dev Allows executing arbitrary calls to authorized target contracts after BLS signature verification.
  1108	     *      The target contract is responsible for its own access control (checking msg.sender == BLSAggregator).
  1109	     *      Accepted residual: unused pre-rotation generic proofs remain owner-executable;
  1110	     *      proposalFloor applies only to slash and aggregator-routed reputation executed through the current DVT.
  1111	     * @param proposalId Unique proposal ID
  1112	     * @param target Target contract to call
  1113	     * @param callData Encoded function call (abi.encodeCall)
  1114	     * @param requiredThreshold Required number of signatures (must be >= minThreshold)
  1115	     * @param proof BLS aggregated signature proof: abi.encode(uint256 signerMask, bytes sigG2)
  1116	     */
  1117	    function executeProposal(
  1118	        uint256 proposalId,
  1119	        address target,
  1120	        bytes calldata callData,
  1121	        uint256 requiredThreshold,
  1122	        bytes calldata proof
  1123	    ) external nonReentrant {
  1124	        // 1. Access Control
  1125	        if (msg.sender != DVT_VALIDATOR && msg.sender != owner()) {
  1126	            revert UnauthorizedCaller(msg.sender);
  1127	        }
  1128	        if (target == address(0)) revert InvalidTarget(target);
  1129	        // H-1: block the slash / consensus-marking selectors on the generic path (any
  1130	        // target) so a caller-chosen (minThreshold) quorum can never drive
  1131	        // SuperPaymaster.queueSlash/executeSlashWithBLS or markProposalExecuted. The
  1132	        // fund-moving slash goes through the per-severity threshold in
  1133	        // verifyAndExecute; the reversible pre-flag has its own dedicated quorum-gated
  1134	        // entry (queueSlashWithConsensus). Reputation (floor-checked branch) and the
  1135	        // GTokenStaking slashes are blocked too; see the H-1 constants. Generic execution
  1136	        // uses a typed replay key and never writes a raw id in this contract or in
  1137	        // DVTValidator. Selector-scoped (not target-scoped) so
  1138	        // Registry.updateOperatorBlacklist still works.
  1139	        if (callData.length >= 4) {
  1140	            bytes4 sel = bytes4(callData[0:4]);
  1141	            if (
  1142	                sel == SEL_QUEUE_SLASH || sel == SEL_EXECUTE_SLASH_BLS || sel == SEL_MARK_PROPOSAL_EXECUTED
  1143	                    || sel == SEL_BATCH_REPUTATION || sel == SEL_STAKING_SLASH || sel == SEL_STAKING_SLASH_BY_DVT
  1144	            ) {
  1145	                revert ForbiddenGenericSelector(sel);
  1146	            }
  1147	        }
  1148	        if (proposalId == 0) revert InvalidProposalId();
  1149	        uint256 replayKey = uint256(keccak256(abi.encode(TAG_PROPOSAL, proposalId)));
  1150	        if (executedProposals[replayKey]) revert ProposalAlreadyExecuted(proposalId);
  1151	        if (requiredThreshold < minThreshold) revert InvalidThreshold();
  1152	        if (requiredThreshold > MAX_VALIDATORS) revert InvalidThreshold();
  1153	
  1154	        // 2. Construct Generic Message Hash (includes requiredThreshold + chainid)
  1155	        bytes32 expectedMessageHash = keccak256(
  1156	            abi.encode(domainSeparator(), TAG_PROPOSAL, proposalId, target, keccak256(callData), requiredThreshold)
  1157	        );
  1158	
  1159	        // 3. Verify BLS Signatures with custom threshold
  1160	        _checkSignatures(proof, expectedMessageHash, requiredThreshold);
  1161	        uint256 signerMask = abi.decode(proof[:32], (uint256));
  1162	        _commitSigners(replayKey, signerMask, expectedMessageHash);
  1163	
  1164	        // 4. Execute Call
  1165	        (bool success, bytes memory returnData) = target.call(callData);
  1166	        if (!success) revert ProposalExecutionFailed(proposalId, returnData);
  1167	
  1168	        // 5. Mark as Executed
  1169	        executedProposals[replayKey] = true;
  1170	
  1171	        emit ProposalExecuted(proposalId, target, keccak256(callData));
  1172	    }
  1173	
  1174	    // ====================================
  1175	    // Internal Functions
  1176	    // ====================================
  1177	
  1178	    /// @dev Validate that a G1 point is:
  1179	    ///      1. Not the identity (point at infinity — all-zero coordinates).
  1180	    ///      2. On the BLS12-381 G1 curve — verified via G1ADD precompile (0x0b)
  1181	    ///         by adding the point to the identity. The precompile rejects points
  1182	    ///         not on the curve with a failed staticcall.
  1183	    ///      3. In the prime-order subgroup of G1 — verified via G1MUL precompile
  1184	    ///         (0x0c) by multiplying by the subgroup order r. A point in the
  1185	    ///         main subgroup must satisfy r*P = O (identity). Points in small
   390	    /**
   391	     * @notice Step 1 of the two-step slash: forward an aggregated queue proof to the
   392	     *         aggregator, which pre-flags the operator (SP.queueSlash) at the
   393	     *         per-severity threshold. Kept separate from execution so the flag lands
   394	     *         in an earlier tx (front-run protection). Not tied to a proposalId —
   395	     *         queue by (operator, level) so it can precede createProposal.
   396	     */
   397	    function queueSlashWithProof(
   398	        address operator,
   399	        uint8 slashLevel,
   400	        uint256 epoch,
   401	        bytes calldata evidence,
   402	        bytes calldata proof
   403	    ) external {
   404	        // Only an active DVT validator may drive a pre-flag. No BLS_AGGREGATOR
   405	        // exemption here (unlike executeWithProof): the aggregator never calls back
   406	        // into this forwarder, so that branch would be dead code.
   407	        _requireActiveValidator(msg.sender);
   408	        _checkEvidence(operator, slashLevel, epoch, evidence);
   409	        IBLSAggregator(BLS_AGGREGATOR).queueSlashWithConsensus(operator, slashLevel, epoch, proof);
   410	    }
   411	
   412	    /**
   413	     * @notice Direct execution with an aggregated proof
   414	     * @dev Shape comes only from stored proposal fields. In BLSAggregator.sol,
   415	     *      lines 1028-1030 reject combined shapes, 1051-1071 select reputation
   416	     *      for non-empty repUsers, 1084-1092 select its Registry update, and
   417	     *      1095-1097 require a nonzero operator to reach the slash branch.
   418	     *      Thus a pure-reputation proposal can never execute a slash.
   419	     * @dev BLSAggregator rejects ids below proposalFloor on this current-DVT path
   420	     *      for slash and aggregator-routed reputation, even when this DVT allocated
   421	     *      the id before rotation and has since been reactivated. Owner-direct
   422	     *      verifyAndExecute with caller-chosen ids remains outside this floor.
   423	     */
   424	    function executeWithProof(
   425	        uint256 id,
   426	        address[] calldata repUsers,
   427	        uint256[] calldata newScores,
   428	        uint256 epoch,
   429	        bytes calldata evidence,
   430	        bytes calldata proof
   431	    ) external onlyAuthorizedExecutor {
   432	        // P0 follow-up: a registered validator that has since exited their
   433	        // role / withdrawn their stake must not be allowed to drive an
   434	        // executeWithProof call, even though the modifier `onlyAuthorizedExecutor`
   435	        // would still treat them as authorized purely on `isValidator[v]`. The
   436	        // BLS aggregator (BLS_AGGREGATOR) is exempted because its own quorum
   437	        // verification re-validates each signing slot independently in
   438	        // BLSAggregator._reconstructPkAgg.
   439	        if (msg.sender != BLS_AGGREGATOR) {
   440	            _requireActiveValidator(msg.sender);
   441	        }
   442	        SlashProposal storage p = proposals[id];
   443	        // P0-17: reject ids that were never created — an unborn id would otherwise
   444	        // be silently forwarded to the aggregator with bogus fields.
   445	        // P0-4 fix: use p.exists instead of operator==address(0) — zero-operator
   446	        // proposals are valid in BLSAggregator (it skips slash for address(0)).
   447	        if (!p.exists) revert ProposalDoesNotExist();
   448	        if (p.executed) revert ProposalExecutedAlready();
   449	
   450	        bytes32 evidenceHash = p.evidenceHash;
   451	        if (p.operator == address(0) && p.slashLevel == 0) {
   452	            if (repUsers.length == 0) revert ProposalShapeMismatch();
   453	            if (evidence.length != 0) revert ReputationEvidenceNotEmpty();
   454	        } else {
   455	            if (repUsers.length != 0 || newScores.length != 0) revert ProposalShapeMismatch();
   456	            evidenceHash = _checkEvidence(p.operator, p.slashLevel, epoch, evidence);
   457	            if (evidenceHash != p.evidenceHash) revert EvidenceHashMismatch();
   458	        }
   459	
   460	        IBLSAggregator(BLS_AGGREGATOR)
   461	            .verifyAndExecute(id, p.operator, p.slashLevel, repUsers, newScores, epoch, evidenceHash, proof);
   462	    }
   463	
   464	    function _checkEvidence(address operator, uint8 level, uint256 epoch, bytes calldata evidence)
   465	        internal
   466	        view
   467	        returns (bytes32 evidenceHash)
   468	    {
   469	        (bytes32 category, bytes memory body) = abi.decode(evidence, (bytes32, bytes));
   470	        address checker = checkerOf[category];
   471	        if (checker == address(0)) revert UnknownEvidenceCategory(category);
   472	
   473	        bytes memory callData = abi.encodeCall(IEvidenceCategory.check, (operator, level, epoch, body));
   474	        bool ok;
   475	        uint256 resultSize;
   476	        uint256 accepted;
   477	        assembly ("memory-safe") {
   478	            ok := staticcall(EVIDENCE_CHECK_GAS, checker, add(callData, 0x20), mload(callData), 0, 0)
   479	            resultSize := returndatasize()
   480	            if and(ok, eq(resultSize, 0x20)) {
   481	                returndatacopy(0, 0, 0x20)
   482	                accepted := mload(0)
   483	            }
   484	        }
   485	        if (!ok || resultSize != 32 || accepted != 1) revert EvidenceRejected(category);
   486	        return keccak256(abi.encode(TAG_EVIDENCE, category, keccak256(body)));
   487	    }
   488	
   489	    function setBLSAggregator(address _bls) external onlyOwner {
   490	        emit BLSAggregatorUpdated(BLS_AGGREGATOR, _bls);
   491	        BLS_AGGREGATOR = _bls;
   492	    }
   493	
   494	    /// @notice Permissionless eviction when a validator no longer meets the
   495	    ///         role + stake requirements (P0 follow-up).
   496	    /// @dev    Anyone can call this. The function reverts (`ValidatorStillEligible`)
   497	    ///         if the validator still has ROLE_DVT AND stake >= minStake — so it
   498	    ///         only succeeds when there's a genuine drift between the local
   499	    ///         flag and on-chain reality. This gives off-chain monitors / public-
   500	    ///         goods bots a way to prune stale validators without waiting for
   501	    ///         the owner. The BLS slot is NOT freed here; use owner-only
   502	    ///         `removeValidator` when BLS voting power must also be revoked (it can revert
   503	    ///         at an exactly-reachable quorum; BLSAggregator.revokeAndSuspend evicts
   504	    ///         unconditionally).
   505	    function pruneValidator(address v) external {
   506	        if (!isValidator[v]) revert NotActiveValidator(v);
   507	        bytes32 roleDvt = keccak256("DVT");
   508	        bool hasRole_ = REGISTRY.hasRole(roleDvt, v);
   509	        IGTokenStaking staking = IRegistryStakingAware(address(REGISTRY)).GTOKEN_STAKING();
   510	        if (address(staking) == address(0)) revert StakingNotConfigured();
   511	        IRegistry.RoleConfig memory cfg = REGISTRY.getRoleConfig(roleDvt);
   512	        (uint128 amount,,,,) = staking.roleLocks(v, roleDvt);
   513	        if (hasRole_ && uint256(amount) >= cfg.minStake) {
   514	            revert ValidatorStillEligible(v);
   515	        }
   516	        isValidator[v] = false;
   517	        emit ValidatorPruned(v, hasRole_, uint256(amount));
   518	    }
   519	
   520	    /// @notice Shared-owner removal: clear local admission and suspend/revoke BLS voting power.
   521	    /// @dev Reverts atomically if a configured aggregator cannot revoke the key.
   522	    function removeValidator(address v) external onlyOwner {
   523	        if (!isValidator[v]) {
   524	            bool active;
   525	            if (BLS_AGGREGATOR != address(0)) {
   526	                (,, active) = IBLSKeyRegistration(BLS_AGGREGATOR).getBLSPublicKey(v);
   527	            }
   528	            if (!active) revert NotActiveValidator(v);
   529	        }
   530	        if (BLS_AGGREGATOR != address(0)) {
   531	            // Only the shared owner may request suspension through this gateway.
   532	            if (IBLSKeyRegistration(BLS_AGGREGATOR).owner() != msg.sender) revert UnauthorizedCaller(msg.sender);
   533	            IBLSKeyRevoker(BLS_AGGREGATOR).revokeBLSPublicKey(v);
   534	        }
   535	        isValidator[v] = false;
   536	        emit ValidatorRemoved(v);
   537	    }
   538	}

   701	        // Bootstrap slash thresholds for N=3: WARNING 2-of-3 (no funds moved,
   702	        // reputation ding only), MINOR/MAJOR 3-of-3 (real aPNTs at stake).
   703	        // Governance raises these via setSlashThreshold as the validator set grows.

   705	        slashThresholds[uint8(ISuperPaymasterSlash.SlashLevel.MINOR)] = 3;
   706	        slashThresholds[uint8(ISuperPaymasterSlash.SlashLevel.MAJOR)] = 3;

   711	        // operator's aPNTs balance; this one protects the guardian's GToken lock, which is
   712	        // the asset the committee-eligibility gate actually reads.
   713	        guardianSlashBps = 3000;

   716	    /// @notice Update the per-severity slash consensus threshold.
   717	    /// @dev    Gated to `slashPolicyAdmin` (a multisig, or a TimelockController for
   718	    ///         timelocked changes). Floored at SLASH_THRESHOLD_FLOOR and capped at
   719	    ///         MAX_VALIDATORS. Operationally the value should stay <= the active
   720	    ///         validator count, otherwise that severity becomes unslashable.
   721	    function setSlashThreshold(uint8 slashLevel, uint8 threshold) external {
   722	        if (msg.sender != slashPolicyAdmin) revert NotSlashPolicyAdmin(msg.sender);
   723	        if (slashLevel > uint8(ISuperPaymasterSlash.SlashLevel.MAJOR)) revert InvalidParameter("slashLevel");
   724	        if (threshold < SLASH_THRESHOLD_FLOOR || threshold > MAX_VALIDATORS) {
   725	            revert SlashThresholdOutOfRange(threshold);
   726	        }
   727	        emit SlashThresholdUpdated(slashLevel, slashThresholds[slashLevel], threshold);
   728	        slashThresholds[slashLevel] = threshold;
   729	    }

   731	    /// @notice Set the fraction of a colluding guardian's lock burned per slash.
   732	    /// @dev    Gated to `slashPolicyAdmin` exactly like `setSlashThreshold`: both decide how
   733	    ///         hard an automated path can hit someone's stake, so they share one admin.
   734	    function setGuardianSlashBps(uint16 newBps) external {
   735	        if (msg.sender != slashPolicyAdmin) revert NotSlashPolicyAdmin(msg.sender);
   736	        if (newBps < GUARDIAN_SLASH_BPS_MIN || newBps > GUARDIAN_SLASH_BPS_MAX) {
   737	            revert InvalidParameter("guardianSlashBps");
   738	        }
   739	        emit GuardianSlashBpsUpdated(guardianSlashBps, newBps);
   740	        guardianSlashBps = newBps;
   741	    }

   750	    // ====================================
   751	    // Core Functions
   752	    // ====================================
   753	
   754	    /// @notice Register a BLS validator's public key into a deterministic slot.
   755	    /// @dev    P0-1: keys are stored uncompressed so `_reconstructPkAgg` can
   756	    ///         feed them straight into the G1ADD precompile. The slot encodes
   757	    ///         the validator's bit position in `signerMask` and is fixed at
   758	    ///         registration to make the bitmap → key mapping unambiguous.
   759	    ///
   760	    ///         P0-1 sub-fix (on-curve + subgroup check): `_validateG1Point` is
   761	    ///         called before storing to guarantee (a) the point is on the
   762	    ///         BLS12-381 G1 curve and (b) it is in the prime-order subgroup r.
   763	    ///         Without (b) an attacker can register a small-subgroup point that
   764	    ///         contaminates the reconstructed pkAgg used in later pairing checks.
   765	    ///         The identity point (point at infinity) is also rejected to prevent
   766	    ///         key-cancellation attacks during aggregation.
   767	    /// @param  validator  validator address (used for events / dedup).
   768	    /// @param  publicKey  uncompressed EIP-2537 G1 point (4×32 bytes).
   769	    /// @param  slot       1-indexed slot in [1..MAX_VALIDATORS]. Must not collide
   770	    ///                    with another validator's already-bound slot.
   771	    /// @param  popSignature proof-of-possession (G2): the validator's BLS signature over
   772	    ///                    `popDigest(validator, publicKey)`. REQUIRED on BOTH paths
   773	    ///                    (CC-48 round-3); a registration without a valid PoP reverts.
   774	    ///
   775	    /// @dev SECURITY: Only the current DVT may forward registration. Both owner
   776	    ///      onboarding and permissionless onboarding require PoP on every call,
   777	    ///      bound to this aggregator, chain, validator address and public key.
   778	    ///      Suspension, key validity, uniqueness and slot consistency are enforced
   779	    ///      here. Key bindings survive revoke/exit unless the owner explicitly
   780	    ///      releases an unused binding (see releaseKeyBinding recovery runbook).
   781	    ///      Aggregation re-checks live role, stake, suspension and matured exits;
   782	    ///      a stored key alone never establishes current voting eligibility.
   783	    function registerBLSPublicKey(
   784	        address validator,
   785	        BLS.G1Point calldata publicKey,
   786	        uint8 slot,
   787	        BLS.G2Point calldata popSignature
   788	    ) external {
   789	        if (msg.sender != DVT_VALIDATOR) revert UnauthorizedCaller(msg.sender);
   790	        if (suspended[validator]) revert ValidatorSuspended(validator);
   791	        if (validator == address(0)) revert InvalidAddress(address(0));
   792	        if (slot == 0 || slot > MAX_VALIDATORS) revert SlotOutOfRange(slot);
   793	
   794	        // CC-48 round-3: proof-of-possession is MANDATORY on BOTH paths. On-curve +
   795	        // prime-order subgroup membership is validated first, so `_verifyPoP` never
   796	        // pairs against a small-subgroup point.
   797	        _validateG1Point(publicKey);
   798	        if (!_verifyPoP(validator, publicKey, popSignature)) revert InvalidPoP();
   799	
   800	        // CC-48 round-2: one public key, one validator address until owner-only release. Enforced on
   801	        // the OWNER path too: the owner is trusted to curate keys, not to be immune to
   802	        // a copy-paste, and a duplicated key silently multiplies one signer's weight.
   803	        bytes32 keyHash = keccak256(abi.encode(publicKey.x_a, publicKey.x_b, publicKey.y_a, publicKey.y_b));
   804	        address keyOwner = blsKeyOwner[keyHash];
   805	        if (keyOwner == address(0)) {
   806	            blsKeyOwner[keyHash] = validator;
   807	        } else if (keyOwner != validator) {
   808	            revert DuplicatePublicKey(keyHash, keyOwner);
   809	        }
   810	
   811	        BLSValidatorKey storage existing = _blsKeys[validator];
   812	        // Re-registration of the SAME validator must reuse the prior slot to
   813	        // avoid leaving a dangling slot pointer for an already-active mask bit.
   814	        if (existing.isActive && existing.index != slot) revert SlotAlreadyTaken(slot);
   815	
   816	        // The slot must either be free, or currently occupied by this same validator.
   817	        address current = validatorAtSlot[slot];
   818	        if (current != address(0) && current != validator) revert SlotAlreadyTaken(slot);
   819	
   820	        _blsKeys[validator] = BLSValidatorKey({publicKey: publicKey, index: slot, isActive: true});
   821	        validatorAtSlot[slot] = validator;
   822	        emit BLSPublicKeyRegistered(validator, slot);
   823	    }
   824	
   825	    /// @notice Revoke a registered key. Direct owner revocation rejects inactive keys.
   826	    /// @dev Current DVT removal is an owner-forwarded suspension plus revocation,
   827	    ///      preserves each reachable configured quorum, and is idempotent for an
   828	    ///      inactive key. The DVT checks that its caller is also this contract's owner.
   829	    ///      Direct owner calls are immediate emergency overrides; no timelock is
   830	    ///      enforced here. Use revokeAndSuspend to prevent permissionless restoration.
   831	    ///      Old key bytes remain inspectable only until a subsequent registration
   832	    ///      overwrites them; getBLSPublicKey is not a historical key archive.
   833	    function revokeBLSPublicKey(address validator) external {
   834	        if (msg.sender != owner() && msg.sender != DVT_VALIDATOR) revert UnauthorizedCaller(msg.sender);
   835	        if (msg.sender == DVT_VALIDATOR) {
   836	            _requireCommitteeSurvivesExit(validator);
   837	            _setSuspended(validator, true);
   838	        } else if (!_blsKeys[validator].isActive) {
   839	            revert KeyNotActive(validator);
   840	        }
   841	        _revokeBLSPublicKey(validator);
   842	    }
   843	
   844	    /// @notice Set or clear suspension regardless of key or admission state.
   845	    function setSuspended(address validator, bool value) external onlyOwner {
   846	        _setSuspended(validator, value);
   847	    }

   849	    /// @notice Atomic emergency eviction; bypasses quorum survival and works without a key.
   850	    function revokeAndSuspend(address validator) external onlyOwner {
   851	        _setSuspended(validator, true);
   852	        _revokeBLSPublicKey(validator);
   853	    }

   855	    function _setSuspended(address validator, bool value) internal {
   856	        suspended[validator] = value;
   857	        emit SuspensionUpdated(validator, value);
   858	    }
